- **dep-fitness**: Use to enforce architecture fitness — no upward or circular crate/package dependencies; the layer ladder and package ownership hold. ([knoxpo/sewlio-studio](https://github.com/knoxpo/sewlio-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/dep-fitness.md))
- **dep-grapher**: Computes inbound and outbound dependencies for a module by running the project's native dependency tooling (cargo tree, npm ls, pip-deptree, go mod graph, mvn dependency:tree, etc.). Returns a structured report. Skips cleanly if no native tool is available. Use during a per-module survey to populate the Dependencies section of CODEBASE.md. ([mtrense/skills](https://github.com/mtrense/skills/blob/HEAD/codebase-survey/agents/dep-grapher.md) · ★9)
- **dep-interceptor**: Check a package name against OSV before install. Fails open — never blocks the user's install. Spawned by PreToolUse hook. ([boredom1234/eco-guardian](https://github.com/boredom1234/eco-guardian/blob/HEAD/claude-plugin/agents/dep-interceptor.md) · ★2)
- **dep-manager**: Dependency Manager. Audits, prunes and pins project dependencies. Kills vulnerable packages before CVEs do. Use when audit trees, license reviews, removing bloat. ([1stANUNNAKI/majlis](https://github.com/1stANUNNAKI/majlis/blob/HEAD/agents/dep-manager.md) · ★2 · 4 copies)
- **dep-mapper**: Subagent of risk-assessor. Given a list of changed files, maps the full dependency graph for the project's stack: who imports or calls these modules, what connects to them through the framework's own wiring (signals, events, middleware, templates, etc.), and what the blast radius is if these files change. Produces a structured blast-radius report. Invoke only from risk-assessor at HIGH+. Projects override this agent with a stack-specific version — this is the generic base. ([thurlow-research/HumanOversightSystem](https://github.com/thurlow-research/HumanOversightSystem/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dep-mapper.md) · ★4)
- **dep-mgr**: 依存パッケージの健全性とセキュリティを維持します。 セマンティックバージョニング、セキュリティ監査、段階的アップグレードを専門とします。 📚 依存スキル (5個): このエージェントは以下のスキルを読み込んでタスクを実行します: - `.claude/skills/semantic-versioning/SKILL.md`: Major/Minor/Patch判断、破壊的変更検出、SemVer範囲指定 - `.claude/skills/dependency-auditing/SKILL.md`: pnpm audit、pnpm audit、CVSS評価、脆弱性トリアージ - `.claude/skills/lock-file-management/SKILL.md`: pnpm-lock.yaml整合性、競合解決、決定性ビルド - `.claude/skills/upgrade-strategies/SKILL.md`: 段階的更新、互換性マトリクス、ロールバック手順 - `.claude/skills/monorepo-dependency-management/SKILL.md`: pnpmワークスペース、バージョン同期、影響分析 Use proactively when tasks relate to dep-mgr responsibilities ([daishiman/AIWorkflowOrchestrator](https://github.com/daishiman/AIWorkflowOrchestrator/blob/HEAD/docs/99-claude/agents/dep-mgr.md) · ★7)

- **dep-reader**: Installed-dependency source reader. Pass a question like "how does &lt;package&gt; implement X" (import or dist name both work). The agent resolves the package's on-disk source with `ccx repo locate`, reads it through token-bounded ccx views, and returns the answer with `path:line#hash` cites — the dependency's source never enters the caller's context. Spawn it for questions about `.venv`, site-packages, vendored, or Go-module dependency internals — the lane the dependency-search guard note points at. - **dep-researcher**: Researches one dependency family across a version range.
